- Revisions: WSP 741: 1929(M). WSP 1302: 1932(M). WSP 1432: 1928-30. WDR NY-85-1: Drainage area. Datum of gage was revised on Sept. 25, 2024, from 308.50 ft to 308.18 ft above NAVD of 1988, based on an updated geodetic survey.

MOHAWK RIVER NEAR LITTLE FALLS NY, USGS site 01347000 (USGS-01347000), stream in Herkimer County, New York, draining 1342 square miles. Daily means from 1927-10-01 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
